Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the fragment Credit Card (1,1) — Owns — (1,N) Customer, drawn in the ER notation of the course. Which of the following statements are true? Select all that apply.

  • A customer may own several credit cards. Пара рядом с Customer, (1,N), считает Owns одного клиента: от одной до многих карт.
  • A credit card may be owned by several customers. Пара рядом с Credit Card — (1,1): одна карта участвует ровно в одном Owns.
  • Every customer owns at least one credit card. Минимум рядом с Customer — 1: обязательное участие.
  • A credit card may have no owner. Минимум рядом с Credit Card — 1, а не 0.
  • The relationship is many-to-many. Максимумы — 1 и N: один-ко-многим, один клиент и много карт.
Показать образец ответа

The first and the third. Each pair describes one instance of the entity next to which it is written: a credit card takes part in exactly one Owns (one owner, never none), a customer in one to N (at least one card, possibly many). The maxima 1 and N make the relationship one-to-many.

Фрагмент — решение самого занятия (p. 1298). Классическая неверная галочка — второй вариант, если прочитать (1,N) как «у карты от 1 до N клиентов», — это перекрёстное прочтение из загадки урока.

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Explain the difference between a key constraint and a participation constraint on a relationship set. How are both expressed in the (min,max) notation used in the course? Give an example.

Показать образец ответа

A key constraint states that an entity takes part in at most one instance of the relationship set: in the lecture's example each department has at most one manager, so Department has a key constraint on Manages, drawn as an arrow from Department to the diamond. A participation constraint states whether every entity must take part in at least one instance: total participation (every department has a manager, drawn as a thick line) or partial participation (there may exist employees managing no department). In the (min,max) pair written next to an entity, the minimum expresses participation (0 partial or optional, 1 total or mandatory) and the maximum expresses the key constraint (1 if the entity takes part at most once, N otherwise). Department (1,1) on Manages means that every department has exactly one manager; Employee (0,N) means that an employee manages zero or more departments.

Полный ответ называет три вещи: max против min, слова total и partial и сторону, на которой записана пара. Упоминание стрелки и жирной линии (pp. 70–71) покажет, что ты читаешь оба диалекта.

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Explain the constraints (T, E), (T, O), (P, E) and (P, O) of an ISA hierarchy using an example. How do they relate to the overlap and covering constraints presented in the lecture?

Показать образец ответа

An ISA hierarchy is constrained along two independent axes. Covering: Total (T) means every instance of the parent belongs to at least one child; Partial (P) means some parent instances may belong to no child. Overlap: Exclusive (E) means no instance belongs to two children; Overlapping (O) means an instance may belong to several. Example: the parent Citizen with the children Hunter and Fisherman. (T, E): every citizen is either a hunter or a fisherman, not both — the children form a partition. (T, O): every citizen is a hunter, a fisherman or both. (P, E): a citizen may be a hunter or a fisherman, not both, or neither. (P, O): any combination, including neither and both. In the lecture's terms, "Hunter AND Fisherman COVER Citizen" is T and "Hunter OVERLAPS Fisherman" is O; without these statements the hierarchy is P and E. The ISA arrow points from the children to the parent and carries no cardinalities.

Назови две оси по отдельности; частый слабый ответ разбирает четыре пары как четыре не связанных между собой случая. Если цитируешь фразу занятия про (T, E), пиши Fisherman, а не Citizen (см. красную ручку).

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Describe the three levels of database design presented in the course. What does each level produce, and why is the conceptual level designed first?

Показать образец ответа

Conceptual design constructs an information model independent from all physical considerations: in the course, an ER diagram with entities, relationships, attributes with their domains, keys and cardinalities. Logical design builds the database on a specific data model: for a relational database it maps the ER schema to relations, normalises them (primary and foreign keys, normal forms up to BCNF) and adds constraints; for a NoSQL system it produces a graph, document or column-family design. Physical design implements the database with specific storage structures and access methods: file organisation, indexes and disk space, chosen after analysing the transactions, plus the security policy. The conceptual level comes first because it commits to no technology: the same diagram can then be mapped to different data models and products, and each later level takes the previous one as its input. The slides compare the three levels to CIM, PIM and PSM in model-driven software engineering.

Три уровня, у каждого свой результат, и причина порядка. Строка про CIM/PIM/PSM напечатана на слайде (p. 63); одного предложения о ней достаточно.

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: a relationship set Works_In may contain two relationships between the same employee and the same department, as long as they have different values of the descriptive attribute since.

  • True Описательные атрибуты не участвуют в идентичности связи.
  • False "A relationship is uniquely identified by participating entities without reference to descriptive attributes" (p. 69).
Показать образец ответа

False. A relationship is uniquely identified by its participating entities, without reference to its descriptive attributes: the pair (employee, department) can appear only once in Works_In, whatever the value of since.

Естественный вопрос «верно/неверно», построенный на последней фразе слайда (p. 69). Исправление — превратить период в сущность — это слайд урока 7 (p. 75).

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the following ER Diagram, which represents a data structure for tracking Formula 1 races (exam of 11 Feb 2025; only the part used here):

  • Race 10:N — Compete — 1:N Pilot; Compete - Winner (Yes/No), Fastest_Lap
  • Ticket 1:1 — Attend — 1:N Race
  • Race 1:1 — On — 1:N Track
  • Team Member 1:N — Include — 1:N Team; Include - Hiring_Date, Resignation_Date
  • Spectator, Team Member, Team Owner ISA Person (T, E); Engineer, Pilot, Mechanic ISA Team Member (P, E)
  • N.B. Each Team Member might have worked for different Teams in different periods of time.

(a) State in one sentence each the constraint expressed by the cardinalities of Compete, Attend and On, and classify each of the three relationships as one-to-one, one-to-many or many-to-many. (b) Explain the two ISA pairs. (c) The N.B. says that a team member may have worked for different teams in different periods. Can Include, as drawn, store this? Which closely related situation can it not store, and why?

Показать образец ответа

(a) Compete: every race has at least ten competing pilots, and every pilot competes in at least one race; both maxima are N, so it is many-to-many. Attend: every ticket is for exactly one race, and every race has at least one ticket; one-to-many, one race with many tickets. On: every race takes place on exactly one track, and every track hosts at least one race; one-to-many, one track with many races.

(b) (T, E) on Person: every person is exactly one of spectator, team member or team owner — a partition. (P, E) on Team Member: a team member is at most one of engineer, pilot and mechanic, and may be none of them.

(c) Yes for different teams: each (member, team) pair is a separate Include instance with its own Hiring_Date and Resignation_Date. But a relationship is identified by its participants, so a member and a team can be linked only once: a member who left a team and later came back cannot be stored with two periods for that team. That would require the employment period to become an entity of its own.

Задания этого варианта опираются на (a): его графовой модели и запросу про "races where at least 15 pilots competed" нужны Compete как связь многие-ко-многим и билет, принадлежащий ровно одной гонке. Пункт (c) — из тех N.B., что меняют проект; исправление, которое предлагает лекция, — в уроке 7.

Урок 6 · Азбука ER-диаграмм на экзамене · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the ER Diagram representing a data structure for tracking natural disasters (exam of 6 Jun 2024; only the part used here):

  • Natural Disaster - ID, …; children Earthquake, Flooding, Landslide, Tornado, ISA labelled T, E
  • Person - Personal ID, …; children Paramedic, Firefighter, Policeman, ISA labelled P, O. Note → A paramedic can be a firefighter (the same applies to the other entities involved in that ISA relationship).
  • Vehicle - Plate, …; children Ambulance, Fire Truck, ISA labelled P, E
  • Cause: a relationship between Natural Disaster and itself, 0:1 on one leg and 0:N on the other, attribute Causal Description (A Natural Disaster may cause another Natural Disaster).

(a) For each hierarchy, describe one population of instances that it forbids, or explain why it forbids none. (b) Read the cardinalities of Cause, stating the assumption you need.

Показать образец ответа

(a) T, E on Natural Disaster forbids a disaster that is none of the four kinds, and a disaster recorded both as an earthquake and as a flooding. P, E on Vehicle forbids a vehicle that is both an ambulance and a fire truck; a vehicle that is neither is allowed. P, O on Person forbids no population: a person may belong to none of the three children or to several of them at once, as the note says.

(b) The diagram does not name the two legs, so I assume that the leg marked 0:1 is the role "caused disaster" and the leg marked 0:N the role "causing disaster". Then every disaster is caused by at most one other disaster, possibly none, and a disaster may cause any number of others, possibly none. With the opposite assumption a disaster would cause at most one other disaster and could have many causes.

Note в варианте — это буква O, записанная словами, а буквы вариант печатает без скобок ("P, O"): смысл тот же. В (b) запиши допущение: диаграмма оставляет выбор открытым, и любое прочтение защитимо, если его сформулировать.

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Explain how you decide whether a concept should be modelled as an attribute or as an entity in an ER diagram. Use the address of an employee as the example.

Показать образец ответа

It depends on how the information is used and on the semantics of the data. An attribute holds one atomic value for its owner. If an employee can have several addresses, address must be an entity connected to Employee by a relationship, because attributes cannot be set-valued. If the structure of the address matters — for example, we want to retrieve the employees who live in a given city — address must also be modelled as an entity, because attribute values are atomic. If neither holds (one address, always used as a whole), an attribute is enough. The same reasoning applies to attributes of relationships: since a relationship instance is identified by its participating entities, several values for the same pair (two working periods of one employee in one department) require a new entity, such as Duration.

Полный ответ даёт два теста с их причинами — "set-valued" и "atomic" — это слова слайда — и решение для каждого случая. Последнее предложение показывает, что ты видишь то же правило за слайдом (p. 75).

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: in an ER diagram, a relationship Works_In between Employee and Department with the attributes from and to can record that the same employee worked in the same department in two different periods.

  • True Атрибуты описывают один экземпляр, а экземпляр на пару (сотрудник, отдел) всего один.
  • False "A relationship is identified by participating entities" (p. 75): второй период той же пары был бы тем же экземпляром. Лекция добавляет сущность Duration и делает связь тернарной.
Показать образец ответа

False. A relationship instance is identified by its participating entities, so there is at most one instance for a given employee and department, with one value of from and to. Several periods require a new entity (Duration) and a ternary relationship.

«Верно/неверно» с возможным штрафом: ловушка в том, что атрибуты выглядят так, будто могут повторяться. Не могут — они висят на паре.

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Describe the top-down, bottom-up and inside-out strategies of conceptual design, and give one primitive of each of the first two.

Показать образец ответа

Top-down: start from a single, abstract schema that covers the whole specification and refine it step by step — initial schema, intermediate schemas, final schema — each step replacing a concept with a more detailed structure; a primitive is "from one entity to two entities and a relationship between them" (others: to a generalization, from one relationship to multiple relationships or to an entity with relationships, adding attributes). Bottom-up: split the specification into components and sub-components, design a schema for each, then integrate the schemas into the final one; a primitive is "generation of an entity" (others: of a relationship, of a generalization, aggregation of attributes on an entity or a relationship). Inside-out: start from the most important concept and grow the schema outward, adding at each step the concepts directly connected to those already present, like an oil stain.

Слайды дают эти стратегии только картинками (pp. 79–83), так что объясни механизм своими словами: уточнение, объединение, рост от ядра. Помни, что bottom-up таблица напечатана под заголовком "Top-down primitives".

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following are primitives of the bottom-up strategy? Select all that apply.

  • Generation of a relationship Bottom-up T2 (p. 82).
  • Aggregation of attributes on an entity Bottom-up T4: разрозненные атрибуты собираются на сущности.
  • From one relationship to an entity with relationships Top-down T4 (p. 80): уточняет существующую связь.
  • Generation of a generalization Bottom-up T3.
  • From one entity to two entities and a relationship between them Top-down T1: уточняет существующую сущность.
Показать образец ответа

Generation of a relationship, aggregation of attributes on an entity, generation of a generalization. The other two refine an existing concept and belong to the top-down strategy.

Практическое правило: "generation" и "aggregation" строят снизу (bottom-up); "from one … to …" уточняет (top-down). Bottom-up строки напечатаны под заголовком "Top-down primitives" (p. 82): знай напечатанный заголовок, а классифицируй по глаголам.

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

In the role-playing-game exercise, a Character has Gold, Experience Points and a Strength Score derived from the strength of the equipped items. Which of these attributes would you keep in the ER model, and why?

Показать образец ответа

An attribute whose value can be computed from data already stored is a derived attribute: storing it is redundant and can become inconsistent. Strength Score can be computed from the strength of the weapons the character wields, so it can be removed; it stays derivable even if it also depends on the Experience Points, since those are stored too. Experience Points can be derived under the assumption that they are only earned: they are then the sum of the experience provided by the monsters the character has defeated, which the model records. Gold must be kept under the assumption that it can be spent: spending is not recorded, so the current amount cannot be recomputed. The decision depends on these assumptions, which must be stated.

Три пункта: определение (производный = вычислимый из хранимых данных, а значит избыточный), по вердикту на атрибут и допущения — слайды печатают и "Yes, they can be derived", и "No, they should be kept", каждый со своим допущением.

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

On the ER diagram of the exam of 11 Feb 2025 (Formula 1 races). Consider this fragment of the diagram: Team Member 1:N — Include — 1:N Team, where Include has the attributes Hiring_Date and Resignation_Date, and the note "N.B. Each Team Member might have worked for different Teams in different periods of time." (a) State the two cardinalities in words. (b) Can the diagram record a mechanic who worked for the same team twice, in two different periods? (c) If not, change the conceptual design so that it can, and give the resulting entities, relationships and cardinalities.

Показать образец ответа

(a) Each team member is included in at least one team and possibly many; each team includes at least one team member and possibly many.

(b) No. A relationship instance is identified by its participating entities, so Include contains at most one instance for the pair (mechanic, team), with one Hiring_Date and one Resignation_Date.

(c) Turn Include into an entity, Employment (Employment_ID, Hiring_Date, Resignation_Date), with Team Member (1,N) — Hold — (1,1) Employment and Employment (1,1) — In — (1,N) Team. Each period is now its own Employment instance, so the same member and team can appear in several periods. Equivalently, add a Period entity identified by Hiring_Date and Resignation_Date and make Include ternary, as the lecture does with Duration.

Пункт (b) — суть N.B.: одна только диаграмма не может удержать ситуацию, которую описывает примечание. В (c) проверь, что новая сущность имеет (1,1) в сторону обеих старых — каждый период работы принадлежит одному участнику и одной команде. Сам вариант (c) не спрашивает; его задание по MongoDB просит документное представление Team и участников команды, и вот там N.B. и кусается: массив периодов внутри каждого участника может перечислить одну и ту же команду дважды (урок 13).

Урок 7 · От текста к диаграмме: проектные решения · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

On the ER diagram of the exam of 6 Sept 2024 (restaurant). The diagram contains Table 1:N — Serve — 1:N Waiter, and the paper adds: "N.B. Tables are served by more Waiters overall, but only one at a time." Explain what the cardinalities express and why the paper needs the N.B. line.

Показать образец ответа

Table 1:N means that each table is served by at least one waiter and possibly many; Waiter 1:N, that each waiter serves at least one table and possibly many. Cardinalities count all the instances of the relationship, whenever they hold, so they cannot say that at any given moment a table has only one waiter: that is a constraint over time. A lot of data semantics can be captured in an ER diagram, but some constraints cannot; they are stated in text, as in this N.B. line, and must be enforced by the database or by the application.

Ответ делают полным две вещи: каждая пара прочитана со своей стороны, и есть ссылка на слайд (p. 73), "some constraints cannot be captured in ER diagrams".

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы»

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: in the relational model a relation may contain two identical tuples, provided that they are stored in different positions of the table.

  • True Отношение — это множество: "the n-tuples are distinct from one another" и "there is no ordering between n-tuples", так что позиций, по которым можно отличить две копии, нет (p. 87).
  • False Отношение — множество кортежей: ни повторов, ни порядка между кортежами (p. 87).
Показать образец ответа

False. A relation is a subset of a cartesian product, i.e. a set of n-tuples: the tuples are distinct from one another and there is no ordering between them. Only inside a tuple are the values ordered (the i-th value comes from the i-th domain), and the relational data model removes even that order by naming the attributes.

Ловушка — «разные позиции»: у кортежей нет позиций. Таблицы SQL могут содержать одинаковые строки, если ключ не объявлен; реляционная модель в определении слайдов — нет.

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Define superkey and key of a relation. Using the relation Students(RegNum, Surname, FirstName, BirthDate, DegreeProg) with the five tuples shown in the lecture, show that {Surname, FirstName, BirthDate} is a key. Why are keys nevertheless defined at the schema level?

Показать образец ответа

A set of attributes K is a superkey for a relation r if r does not contain two distinct tuples t1 and t2 with t1[K] = t2[K]. K is a key if it is a minimal superkey: no proper subset of K is a superkey.

The five tuples have five different (Surname, FirstName, BirthDate) triples, so the triple is a superkey. Each proper subset repeats a value: {Surname, FirstName} has (Black, Lucy) twice, {Surname, BirthDate} has (Smith, 29/04/59) twice, {FirstName, BirthDate} has (Lucy, 01/05/61) twice, and the single attributes repeat as well. Hence the triple is minimal: a key — of this instance.

Constraints correspond to properties of the real world, so they are associated with the schema and must hold in every legal instance. An individual instance can satisfy other uniqueness properties by chance (here {FirstName, BirthDate, DegreeProg} is also unique), and it can refute a key but never prove one.

В полном ответе три вещи: "distinct tuples" в определении суперключа, слово "minimal" с контрпарой для каждого подмножества из двух атрибутов и мысль о схеме против экземпляра со слайда (p. 97).

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which types of null value does the lecture distinguish, and which one do DBMSs adopt? Why are nulls not allowed in the primary key?

Показать образец ответа

At least three: the unknown value (a domain value exists, but it is not known); the non-existent value (the attribute is not applicable for the tuple); the no-information value (we do not know whether a value exists or not — the logical or of the other two). DBMSs do not distinguish between them and implicitly adopt the no-information value.

The primary key is the key chosen to identify the tuples and to realise the references between relations. A tuple with a null in its key could neither be told apart from the others nor be referenced, so for each relation one key is selected as primary key and nulls are not allowed on it; its attributes are underlined.

Полный ответ называет три вида, «логическое или» (logical or) и фразу "no-information value" для того, что делают СУБД. Вторая половина спрашивает о причине, а не просто «PK is NOT NULL».

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Define a referential constraint. In the database of the lecture — Offences(Code, Date, Officer, Dept, Registration), Officers(RegNum, Surname, FirstName), Cars(Registration, Dept, Owner) with primary key (Registration, Dept) — say which of these operations violates a referential constraint and why: (a) delete officer 638; (b) delete officer 456; (c) change the Dept of offence 630876 from 47 to 75; (d) set the car of offence 143256 to (5694 FR, 47).

Показать образец ответа

A referential constraint imposes that the values on a set X of attributes of a relation R1 appear as values of the primary key of another relation R2. Here Offences.Officer refers to Officers and Offences.(Registration, Dept) refers to Cars.

(a) No violation: no offence references officer 638, and the constraint does not require every officer to be referenced. (b) Violation: offences 987554, 987557 and 630876 reference 456 and would refer to nothing. (c) No violation: (6544 XY, 75) is a row of Cars — although the offence now refers to Cordon's car instead of Mimault's: the constraint checks existence, not correctness. (d) Violation: 5694 FR and 47 both occur in Cars, but not in the same row; the foreign key is the pair, and (5694 FR, 47) is not a primary-key value of Cars.

Четыре случая проверяют два свойства: направление ((a) против (b)) и составной ключ, сопоставляемый целиком ((d)). Тонкий случай — (c): допустимый экземпляр не обязательно истинный.

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

An ER diagram contains Sale (1,1) — yields — (0,1) Cash Receipt. According to the conversion rules of the lecture, how is the relationship represented in the relational schema?

  • The primary key of Sale is posted into the Cash Receipt table Cash Receipt — (0,1): чек, не связанный ни с одной продажей (CR4 на слайде, p. 112), получает NULL — "If you post the opposite direction, either redundancy … OR load … will be a problem".
  • The primary key of Cash Receipt is posted into the Sale table Шаг 4: переносим в таблицу сущности (1,1). Каждая продажа даёт ровно один чек, так что в столбце нет ни NULL, ни списков.
  • A separate table yields(SaleID, CR-ID) Шаг 4 говорит "Do NOT make a separate table".
  • Both keys are posted, one into each table Каждая связь хранилась бы дважды: один факт в двух местах.
Показать образец ответа

CR-ID is posted into Sale as a foreign key (Step 4: post the related entity's primary key into the (1,1) entity's table). The column is fully loaded and single-valued. Posting SaleID into Cash Receipt would create nulls for the receipts that come from no sale.

Вопрос с одним верным ответом, где каждый неверный вариант — отдельное правило со слайдов (pp. 104–110): направление, отдельная таблица, перенос в обе стороны.

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Purchase (0,1) — pays for — (0,1) Cash Disbursement. There are 18 purchases and 26 cash disbursements; 14 purchases are paid, each by a different disbursement. Compute the load, in percent, of the foreign-key column if Check# is posted into the Purchase table.

Показать образец ответа
\[ \text{load} = \frac{14}{18} \approx 77.8\,\% \]

In the other direction, Receiving Report# posted into Cash Disbursement has 14 non-null values out of 26, about 53.8 %. Both maxima are 1, so both directions are allowed; for (0,1)-(0,1) Step 5 posts in the direction of the highest load, provided it is high. 77.8 % is high: Check# goes into Purchase, as a nullable column.

Для второго направления слайд печатает 13/26 (см. красную ручку в разделе 5); решение то же. Ответ 78 принимается.

Урок 8 · Реляционная модель и рецепт «ER → таблицы» · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the following part of the ER diagram of the exam of 13 June 2025, which tracks concert attendances and performances (the exercise itself is ours). Entities: Maestro – Personal ID, Name, Surname, Birth Date, Biography, Stage Name; Orchestra – Name, Foundation Date, Biography; Musician – Personal ID, Name, Surname, Birth Date, Biography; Instrument – Name, Type, Description; Concert – ID, Title, Start Time, Duration; Location – Name, Address, Type, N Seats. (In the paper Maestro and Musician inherit the first four attributes from Person; treat them as plain entities.) Relationships: Direct – First Concert (Yes/No): Maestro (1,1) – Orchestra (1,1); Compose: Orchestra (10,N) – Musician (1,2); Play – Since: Musician (1,N) – Instrument (1,N); Located: Concert (1,1) – Location (1,N).

Write the relational schema: one line per table, primary keys underlined, foreign keys marked with *. For each relationship, name the conversion step you apply.

Показать образец ответа

Maestro(PersonalID, Name, Surname, BirthDate, Biography, StageName)
Orchestra(Name, FoundationDate, Biography, MaestroID*, FirstConcert)
Musician(PersonalID, Name, Surname, BirthDate, Biography)
Instrument(Name, Type, Description)
Compose(OrchestraName*, MusicianID*)
Play(MusicianID*, InstrumentName*, Since)
Location(Name, Address, Type, NSeats)
Concert(ID, Title, StartTime, Duration, LocationName*, LocationAddress*)

Direct is (1,1)-(1,1): Step 3. A maestro and an orchestra are conceptually separate, so one key is posted into the other table — here the maestro's into Orchestra; the opposite direction is equally correct, but not both, and no separate table. First Concert is posted alongside the foreign key. Compose: the maximum 2 of Musician is more than 1, so a single cell cannot hold a musician's orchestras; both maxima count as "many" and Step 2 applies: a separate table with the composite primary key. Play is (1,N)-(1,N): Step 2, and Since goes into the Play table. Located is (1,1)-(1,N): Step 4, Location's key is posted into Concert — a composite foreign key (Name, Address), matched as a pair.

Проверь в своём ответе правильный шаг для каждой связи, подчёркнутые составные ключи, место First Concert и Since и составной внешний ключ. Ловушка — (1,2): для правил переноса 2 считается как «много». Этого нет на слайдах, но если есть время, стоит одного предложения: минимум 10 у (10,N) и максимум 2 у (1,2) эти таблицы не выражают, потому что ни ключ, ни внешний ключ считать не умеют.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Define a functional dependency X → Y on a relational table R. Explain why the primary key of R functionally determines every column of R, and why the rows stored in R can show that a functional dependency does not hold but can never show that it holds.

Показать образец ответа

Y is functionally dependent on X if each value of X in R is associated with precisely one value of Y at any given time: any two rows that agree on X also agree on Y. X is called the determinant. A primary key identifies exactly one row, so each key value is associated with one value of every column, and every column depends on the key; conversely, a set of columns that determines all columns is a superkey. A functional dependency is a constraint on every legal state of the table, asserted by the designer from the meaning of the data — for example "each drinker has one address". One pair of rows with the same X and different Y refutes it; rows that agree with it only show that the current instance is consistent with it, since a later row may break it.

В полном ответе три части: определение ("precisely one value of Y at any given time" слайдов или форма с двумя строками), аргумент про ключ и различие между экземпляром и правилом. Ответ, который «проверяет FD на данных», упускает третье.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Consider the table Employee(EmpID, EmpName, Payrate, Hours Worked, Dept#, DeptName) with primary key EmpID, where every employee works in one department and every department has one name. In which normal form is the table?

  • It is not in 1NF В каждой ячейке одно значение, и у строк есть ключ: это отношение, значит, оно в 1НФ.
  • In 1NF but not in 2NF Частичной зависимости нужен составной ключ; EmpID — один столбец, так что зависеть не от чего, кроме всего ключа.
  • In 2NF but not in 3NF EmpID → Dept# → DeptName — транзитивная зависимость через неключевой Dept#: DeptName — факт об отделе, повторённый у каждого сотрудника.
  • In 3NF 3НФ запрещает транзитивные зависимости, а DeptName зависит от ключа только через Dept#.
Показать образец ответа

In 2NF but not in 3NF. The single-column key rules out partial dependencies, but EmpID → Dept# and Dept# → DeptName make DeptName transitively dependent on the key. Splitting off Department(Dept#, DeptName) and keeping Dept# in Employee as a foreign key gives 3NF.

Это таблица со слайда (p. 117) ("Fixing One Fact Multiple Places", урок 8): обе половины лекции задают один и тот же вопрос. Ловушка — вариант 2: «факт не на своём месте» звучит как частичная зависимость, но при ключе из одного столбца частичной не может быть ничего.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements are true? Select all that apply.

  • Every table in BCNF is also in 3NF. НФБК требует, чтобы каждый детерминант был суперключом; 3НФ это принимает — и не только это.
  • Every table in 3NF is also in BCNF. Неверно: TEACHING(Student, Course, Teacher) с Teacher → Course находится в 3НФ, потому что Course входит в потенциальный ключ, но не в НФБК, потому что Teacher — не суперключ.
  • A table in 1NF whose only candidate key is a single column is in 2NF. Частичным зависимостям нужна часть составного ключа; при одном ключевом столбце её нет. (Слайды говорят "primary key"; "only candidate key" делает утверждение точным.)
  • A table whose cells hold lists of values is in 1NF as long as it has a primary key. Неверно: 1НФ требует атомарных значений — "no sets of values within a column" (p. 128); такая таблица — "NOT a relation" (p. 129).
  • A transitive dependency is a dependency of a non-key column on part of a composite primary key. Неверно: это частичная зависимость. Транзитивная идёт через другой неключевой столбец.
Показать образец ответа

True: "Every table in BCNF is also in 3NF" and "A table in 1NF whose only candidate key is a single column is in 2NF". The other three are false: 3NF does not imply BCNF; a table with lists in its cells is not in 1NF; the definition given is that of a partial dependency.

Вопрос «выбери все верные», где за неверную галочку могут оштрафовать: отмечай только то, что можешь обосновать одной строкой. Пара «частичная против транзитивной» — самая частая путаница в этой теме.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

What are the goals of normalization? Using a table that stores orders, customers and products in the same rows, explain the update, insertion and deletion anomalies that normalization removes.

Показать образец ответа

Normalization aims at a set of relational tables that are free of redundant data and can be consistently and correctly modified: it eliminates redundant data and ensures that each table only stores related data, which saves space and makes the data easier to maintain; the target is 3NF. In INVOICE(Order_ID, Product_ID, Order_Date, Customer_ID, Customer_Name, Customer_Address, Product_Description, Product_Finish, Unit_Price, Ordered_Quantity) with key (Order_ID, Product_ID): update anomaly — the customer's address is repeated on every line of every order, so a change of address must be applied to many rows or the table becomes inconsistent; insertion anomaly — a new product that nobody has ordered cannot be inserted, because Order_ID is part of the key and cannot be NULL; deletion anomaly — deleting the only order line of a product also deletes the product's description and price. Splitting the table into ORDER_LINE, PRODUCT, ORDER and CUSTOMER stores each fact once and removes all three.

Слайды дают цели (pp. 121, 127), но не названия аномалий — они из учебников; полный ответ даёт и фразу о цели, и по одному конкретному примеру на каждую аномалию. Счёт Pine Valley — естественный пример.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Consider the restaurant ER diagram of the exam of 6 Sep 2024: Waiter (a Person, key Personal_ID, with Surname and Hiring Date) is related by Serve (1:N on both sides) to Table (key Table_ID, with Max Seats), and Table is related by Has (Table 1:1, Restaurant 1:N) to Restaurant (key Restaurant_ID, with Name). The Serve relationship has been exported to a single table:

SERVICE(Personal_ID, Table_ID, Surname, Hiring_Date, Max_Seats, Restaurant_ID, Restaurant_Name), primary key (Personal_ID, Table_ID).

(a) List the functional dependencies that follow from the ER diagram and classify each as full, partial or transitive. (b) In which normal form is SERVICE? (c) Decompose it into 3NF, marking primary and foreign keys.

Показать образец ответа

(a) Personal_ID → Surname, Hiring_Date: partial (part of the key). Table_ID → Max_Seats, Restaurant_ID: partial; Restaurant_ID depends on Table_ID because every table belongs to exactly one restaurant. Restaurant_ID → Restaurant_Name: transitive, since the key reaches Restaurant_Name only through the non-key Restaurant_ID. No non-key column depends fully on the whole key: Serve has no attributes.

(b) 1NF (atomic values, a key), not 2NF (partial dependencies).

(c) 2NF: WAITER(Personal_ID, Surname, Hiring_Date), TABLE(Table_ID, Max_Seats, Restaurant_ID, Restaurant_Name), SERVE(Personal_ID*, Table_ID*). TABLE still has a transitive dependency, so for 3NF split it: TABLE(Table_ID, Max_Seats, Restaurant_ID*) and RESTAURANT(Restaurant_ID, Restaurant_Name). Final schema: WAITER, SERVE, TABLE, RESTAURANT; underlined = primary key, * = foreign key.

Сценарий — ER из экзамена про ресторан; таблица придумана для этого урока. Полезная самопроверка: результат совпадает с тем, что сразу даёт рецепт перевода ER в таблицы из урока 8, — Serve, многие-ко-многим, становится отдельной таблицей с составным ключом; Has, где Table на стороне (1,1), переносит Restaurant_ID в TABLE.

Урок 9 · Нормальные формы: ключ, весь ключ и ничего, кроме ключа · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

State the definition of Boyce–Codd normal form. How does it differ from third normal form, and why do the lecture slides call it "3.5NF"? Give an example of a table that is in 3NF but not in BCNF.

Показать образец ответа

A table R is in BCNF if, for every functional dependency X → Y, either the dependency is trivial (Y ⊆ X) or X is a superkey of R. 3NF requires, for every non-trivial X → A, that X is a superkey or that A belongs to some candidate key; BCNF drops this second alternative, so it is slightly stronger, and every BCNF table is in 3NF — hence "3.5NF", between 3NF and 4NF. The two differ only when a table has overlapping candidate keys. Example: TEACHING(Student, Course, Teacher) with {Student, Course} → Teacher and Teacher → Course; the candidate keys are {Student, Course} and {Student, Teacher}. Teacher → Course violates BCNF because Teacher is not a superkey, but satisfies 3NF because Course is part of a candidate key. Decomposing into (Teacher, Course) and (Student, Teacher) reaches BCNF but loses the dependency {Student, Course} → Teacher, which is why 3NF is often the practical target.

Определение почти дословно взято со слайда (p. 139); пример и аргумент о сохранении зависимостей — из учебников, на которые слайды ссылаются (p. 140). Пример показывает, что ты понимаешь разницу, а не повторяешь её.

Модуль 3 · Графовые базы данных

19 вопросов · Лекция 6 и занятие E02

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

What is index-free adjacency? Explain why it makes traversing relationships in a graph database cheaper than joins in a relational database, and say what indexes are used for in Neo4j.

Показать образец ответа

A graph database "provides index-free adjacency": every node stores direct references to its adjacent relationships and, through them, to its neighbouring nodes. Moving from a node to its neighbours is therefore a pointer dereference, whose cost depends on that node's relationships and not on the size of the whole graph. In a relational database a relationship is a foreign key, and following it is a join: for each row an index built over the whole table is searched, at a cost that grows with the table (about log n), and this is repeated at every hop while the intermediate results grow, since friends share friends. For deep or variable-depth traversals, such as friends of friends of friends, the graph database does far less work; at one hop there is no difference. In Neo4j indexes are used only to find the starting nodes of a query; from there the traversal follows the stored relationships.

Ответ держится на трёх вещах: определение словами слайда (index-free adjacency, p. 252), причина (шаг против поиска, чья цена растёт с таблицей) и роль индексов (начальные точки, p. 272). Измеренные времена из загадки — бонус; если их приводишь, скажи, что они из книги Graph Databases.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Compare property graphs (Neo4j) and directed labelled graphs (RDF). Where do the attributes live in each model, and how would each store the day of a reservation that links a sailor to a boat?

Показать образец ответа

A property graph is made of nodes, relationships and properties. Nodes carry zero or more labels and key-value properties; relationships are directed, have exactly one type and can carry properties too. The reservation is one relationship with a property: (:Sailor {sid: 22})-[:RESERVES {day: date('1996-10-10')}]->(:Boat {bid: 101}). An RDF graph is a set of subject–predicate–object triples whose subjects and predicates are IRIs: every attribute is itself an edge from a resource to a literal (:s22 :sname "dustin"), and a label is an rdf:type triple. A relationship is just a triple and cannot carry an attribute, so to store the day the reservation must become a resource of its own (reification): :res1 :sailor :s22, :res1 :boat :b101, :res1 :day "1996-10-10". Property graphs are queried with Cypher and built as application databases with fast traversals; RDF is queried with SPARQL and built to publish and link data through shared identifiers.

Это пункт теории из указаний слово в слово: "Property graphs (neo4j)", "Direct labelled graphs (RDF)". Слайды рисуют стиль RDF (pp. 255–257), не называя его, так что сильный ответ опирается на контраст — где живут атрибуты, что происходит со связью, у которой есть атрибут, — а не на синтаксис RDF.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

According to the lecture, which of the following describe Neo4j? Select all that apply.

  • It is schema free: data does not have to adhere to any convention. "Neo4j is schema free – Data does not have to adhere to any convention" (p. 263).
  • It is especially efficient for whole-graph analysis, such as ranking every node of a large graph. Наоборот: "Not so efficient in whole-graph analysis" (p. 265). Index-free adjacency помогает локальным обходам, а не вычислениям, которые посещают всё.
  • It provides ACID transactions. "ACID – atomic, consistent, isolated and durable for logical units of work" (p. 263), повторено на слайде (p. 265).
  • It searches an index at every hop of a traversal. Индексы находят только начальные точки (p. 272); переходы идут по хранимым связям — index-free adjacency (p. 252).
  • It is meant to be an operational database, not specifically for analytics. Первая строка слайда "Purpose" (p. 265).
  • Each node has exactly one label. У узла их может быть несколько: (n:Actor:Director {name:'Clint Eastwood'}) (p. 269).
Показать образец ответа

Schema free, ACID, and meant to be an operational database. Neo4j is "Not so efficient in whole-graph analysis", uses indexes only to find the starting nodes of a query, and allows several labels per node.

Три верных варианта из шести. Когда неверная галочка может стоить баллов, безопасные галочки — фразы, напечатанные на слайдах (pp. 263 и 265); неверные варианты — три классические путаницы: аналитика, индексы, метки.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

How many edges does the complete undirected graph \(K_7\) have?

  • 42 Это \(n(n-1)\), формула слайда: она считает упорядоченные пары, стрелки полного ориентированного графа.
  • 49 \(7^2\) ещё и сопоставляет каждую вершину самой себе; в полном графе петель нет.
  • 21 По одному ребру на каждую неупорядоченную пару из 7 вершин: \(7 \cdot 6 / 2 = 21\).
  • 28 \(8 \cdot 7 / 2 = 28\) — это \(K_8\), на одну вершину больше.
Показать образец ответа
\[ |E(K_7)| = \frac{7 \cdot 6}{2} = 21 \]

Every unordered pair of the 7 vertices is one edge.

Слайд печатает "Has n(n-1) edges", что даёт 42: стрелки полного ориентированного графа. На его же рисунке \(K_4\) 6 рёбер. Если закрытый вопрос предлагает и 21, и 42, выбирай по графу: 21 — ненаправленный, 42 — с обоими направлениями.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: the query MATCH (user)-[:FRIEND]-(friend) WITH user, count(friend) AS friends ORDER BY friends DESC SKIP 1 LIMIT 3 RETURN user returns the three users with the most friends.

  • True SKIP 1 сначала отбрасывает пользователя с наибольшим числом друзей; потом LIMIT 3 оставляет следующих троих.
  • False "SKIP skips results at the top and LIMIT limits the number of results": пользователи со второго по четвёртое место.
Показать образец ответа

False. WITH counts the friends of each user, ORDER BY sorts the users by that count in descending order, SKIP 1 drops the first one and LIMIT 3 keeps the next three: the users ranked second to fourth.

Запрос — это "General Query Format" со слайда (p. 276) слово в слово. Вопрос «верно/неверно» проверяет одно предложение за раз; здесь ловушка — SKIP. У пользователей с одинаковым числом друзей порядок не определён, если не добавить второй ключ сортировки.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the Sailors example of the lecture (p. 254) as an ER model. The following attributes describe the entities; the primary keys are underlined. Sailor - sid (integer), sname, rating (integer), age (real). Boat - bid (integer), bname, color. The relationship Reserve links Sailor (0,N) and Boat (0,N) and has the attribute day (a date).

Suppose you want to store the respective data instances in a graph database. Provide a written textual representation of the graph model, using the typical Neo4J notation for nodes ( ), edges -[ ]->, and attributes { }. Include data types for attributes. Then write one example instance: sailor 22 (dustin, rating 7, age 45.0) reserved boat 101 (Interlake, red) on 10/10/96.

Показать образец ответа 
(:Sailor {sid: Integer, sname: String,
          rating: Integer, age: Float})
(:Boat {bid: Integer, bname: String, color: String})
(:Sailor)-[:RESERVES {day: Date}]->(:Boat)
(:Sailor {sid: 22, sname: "dustin", rating: 7, age: 45.0})
  -[:RESERVES {day: date("1996-10-10")}]->
(:Boat {bid: 101, bname: "Interlake", color: "red"})

Reserve is not a node: it is the relationship, and its attribute day is a property of the edge. There is no sid or bid on the edge either: the edge itself links the sailor and the boat, where the relational schema of the lecture needed them as foreign keys.

Формулировка взята из экзамена 30 Jan 2026 (там 2 PT) — это первая часть каждой задачи по Neo4j; в 2024–2025 это был «набросок» (sketch) на 1 балл. Схема оценивания не опубликована, но вот что идёт против рецепта практических занятий (p. 1385) или против вопроса: узел Reserve, sid и bid, повторённые на ребре, связь без типа или без направления, пропущенные типы данных. В прошлых экзаменах даты пишут как dd/mm/yyyy: храни их как Date и укажи, какой формат предполагаешь.

Урок 10 · Зачем графы: property graphs, RDF и Neo4j · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider an ER model of football transfers, the data imported on p. 271. The following attributes describe the entities; the primary keys are underlined. Player - Player_ID, Name, Position. Club - Club_ID, Name. Transfer - Transfer_ID, Season, Rank, Fee. Relationships: Of_Player links Transfer (1,1) and Player (0,N) and has the attribute Age, the player's age at the time of the transfer; From_Club and To_Club each link Transfer (1,1) and Club (0,N): the club that sells and the club that buys.

Provide a written textual representation of the graph model, using the typical Neo4J notation for nodes ( ), edges -[ ]->, and attributes { }, with data types. Explain why Transfer is a node and not a relationship.

Показать образец ответа 
(:Player {player_id: String, name: String, position: String})
(:Club {club_id: String, name: String})
(:Transfer {transfer_id: String, season: String,
            rank: Integer, fee: Float})
(:Transfer)-[:OF_PLAYER {age: Integer}]->(:Player)
(:Transfer)-[:FROM_CLUB]->(:Club)
(:Transfer)-[:TO_CLUB]->(:Club)

A transfer links three nodes — a player, a selling club and a buying club — and has attributes of its own, while a Neo4j relationship joins exactly two nodes; so the transfer becomes a node with three typed relationships. The player's age belongs to the link between the transfer and the player and is a property of OF_PLAYER. The two relationships to Club are told apart by their types, FROM_CLUB and TO_CLUB.

Это модель, стоящая за импортом со слайда (p. 271). Дизайн вроде (:Club)-[:TRANSFERS {player: …, fee: …}]->(:Club) теряет игрока как узел: ты больше не сможешь пройти от игрока к его трансферам. Тот же ход — сущность с несколькими партнёрами остаётся узлом — встречается в прошлых экзаменах, например Ticket на экзамене 11 Feb 2025; иерархии ISA и остальной рецепт — урок 12.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Scenario (the restaurant ER of the exam of 6 Sep 2024): Person (Personal_ID, Name, Surname, …) with the child entity Waiter (Hiring Date); Table (Table_ID, Table Number, Max Seats); Dish (Dish_ID, Name, Price, Recipe, Description). A waiter serves tables (Serve); dishes are ordered at tables (Order). Assume this graph model:

(:Person:Waiter {personal_id: String, name: String, …})
  -[:SERVE]->(:Table {table_id: String, max_seats: Integer, …})
(:Table)-[:ORDER]->(:Dish {dish_id: String, name: String, …})

Write a Cypher query to count the total number of different dishes ordered at tables whose max seats is greater than 10, considering only tables served by at least one waiter whose name is "Federico".

Показать образец ответа 
MATCH (w:Waiter {name: "Federico"})-[:SERVE]->(t:Table)
      -[:ORDER]->(d:Dish)
WHERE t.max_seats > 10
RETURN count(DISTINCT d) AS differentDishes

Адаптировано из упражнения 2 той работы, которое спрашивает "for each table" и поэтому требует группировки (урок 12). Что в ответе должно быть верно: один шаблон для цепочки официант → стол → блюдо, равенство внутри узла, диапазон в WHERE и DISTINCT. Тестовый граф: два Federico обслуживают стол 1 (12 мест; паста и рыба), один из них ещё и стол 2 (16 мест; рыба и салат). Шаблон даёт 6 строк для 3 разных блюд; count(d) ответил бы 6.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Scenario (the natural-disasters ER of the exam of 6 Jun 2024): Natural Disaster (ID, Start Time, Start Date, End Time, End Date, Disaster Description) with the total, exclusive children Earthquake (Magnitude, Mercalli Scale), Flooding (Water Height), Landslide (Landslide Class) and Tornado (EF Scale); the recursive relationship Cause ("A Natural Disaster may cause another Natural Disaster") with the attribute Causal Description. Assume each disaster is one node carrying both labels, and Cause is a relationship from the causing disaster to the caused one:

(:NaturalDisaster:Earthquake {id: String, magnitude: Float, …})
(:NaturalDisaster)-[:CAUSE {causal_description: String}]
  ->(:NaturalDisaster)

Write a Cypher query to return the earthquakes with a magnitude greater than 8 that caused at least one flooding with the causal description equal to "very strong earthquake", which in turn caused at least one landslide.

Показать образец ответа 
MATCH (e:Earthquake)-[c:CAUSE]->(f:Flooding)
      -[:CAUSE]->(:Landslide)
WHERE e.magnitude > 8
  AND c.causal_description = "very strong earthquake"
RETURN DISTINCT e

Адаптировано из упражнения 3 той работы, где это запрос MongoDB ("which in turn caused two landslides" требует подсчёта: урок 12). Первой связи нужна переменная, потому что условие стоит на её свойстве; оползень остаётся анонимным, потому что из него ничего не возвращают. DISTINCT — потому что наводнение, вызвавшее два оползня, возвращает своё землетрясение дважды; ровно это и сделал тестовый граф без него.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Scenario: the same natural-disasters ER and graph model as the previous question.

Write a Cypher query to return the ID and the description of every natural disaster caused, directly or through a chain of at most three Cause relationships, by the natural disaster with ID "ND-01".

Показать образец ответа 
MATCH (d:NaturalDisaster {id: "ND-01"})
      -[:CAUSE*1..3]->(x:NaturalDisaster)
RETURN DISTINCT x.id, x.disaster_description

Здесь важны три вещи: ограниченный шаблон переменной длины с типом перед звёздочкой, стрелка и DISTINCT. На тестовой цепочке ND-01 → ND-02 → ND-03 → ND-04 → ND-05, плюс ND-01 → ND-06 и ND-07 → ND-01, ответ — ND-02, ND-03, ND-04 и ND-06. Убери наконечник — и проскочит ND-07, бедствие, которое вызвало ND-01; убери границу — и проскочит ND-05, до которого четыре шага. DISTINCT ничего не стоит и защищает тебя, как только две цепочки могут дойти до одного и того же бедствия.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Scenario (the hospital-heating ER of the exam of 30 Jan 2026): Building (Building_ID, Name, Year), Floor (Floor_ID, Number, Has_Elevator), Heating System (Heater_Sys_ID, Heating_Power) with the (T, E) children HVAC System and Gas Heating System; Has Floor (Building – Floor) and Heats (Heating System – Floor, with Start_Time and End_Time). Assume this graph model:

(:Building {building_id: String, name: String, year: Integer})
  -[:HAS_FLOOR]->(:Floor {floor_id: String, number: Integer,
                          has_elevator: Boolean})
(:HeatingSystem:GasHeatingSystem {heater_sys_id: String, …})
  -[:HEATS {start_time: DateTime, end_time: DateTime}]->(:Floor)

Write a Cypher query that returns the buildings built after 1967 with at least one floor with an elevator that is heated by a Gas Heating System. Return the ID and the name of each building once.

Показать образец ответа 
MATCH (b:Building)-[:HAS_FLOOR]->(f:Floor)
      <-[:HEATS]-(:GasHeatingSystem)
WHERE b.year > 1967 AND f.has_elevator = true
RETURN DISTINCT b.building_id, b.name

Адаптировано из упражнения 1 той работы (её запрос суммирует кровати, а это требует группировки). Шаблон — та же «галочка» (V) партнёров по съёмкам со слайда (p. 1358), только посередине этаж, а дочерняя метка :GasHeatingSystem сама по себе выбирает газовые системы. Без DISTINCT тестовое здание вернулось трижды: две газовые системы отапливают один его этаж и одна — другой.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

On the dataset of the exercise session, this query returns 3:

MATCH (js:Person)-[:KNOWS]-()-[:KNOWS]-(surfer)
WHERE js.name = "Johan" AND surfer.hobby = "surfing"
RETURN COUNT(surfer)

The same query with RETURN COUNT(DISTINCT surfer) returns 1. Explain why, and say which number answers "how many people can teach Johan to surf".

Показать образец ответа

MATCH produces one row for every way the pattern fits the graph, that is one row per path. The only surfer, Allison, is reached from Johan by three different paths of two KNOWS relationships: through Ian over the relationship Johan→Ian, through Ian over the relationship Ian→Johan (the pattern has no direction, and these are two different relationships), and through Rik. Within one path a relationship is never used twice, but different paths may end at the same node. COUNT(surfer) counts the rows, so 3; COUNT(DISTINCT surfer) counts the different nodes, so 1. The number of people is 1. The PROFILE plan shows it: 3 rows enter the Distinct operator and 1 leaves it.

Полный ответ опирается на две идеи: строки — это пути, а не узлы, и две связи KNOWS между Johan и Ian считаются разными путями. Если назвать три пути, расплывчатый ответ становится точным; числа PROFILE его подкрепляют.

Урок 11 · Cypher: узлы, связи, паттерны и пути · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

True or false: in Neo4j, inserting WITH p between MATCH …, p = shortestPath((a)-[*]-(b)) and WHERE length(p) > 1 never changes the result of the query.

  • True Без WITH предикат принадлежит шаблону кратчайшего пути, и исчерпывающий fallback планируется; с WITH это обычный фильтр, применённый к одному уже выбранному пути.
  • False Если кратчайший путь имеет длину 1, а более длинный существует, запрос без WITH вернёт более длинный путь, а запрос с WITH не вернёт ничего.
Показать образец ответа

False. Without WITH, the predicate is evaluated as part of the shortest-path pattern; because it needs the whole path, Neo4j plans the slower exhaustive depth-first search as a fallback, so the query returns the shortest path of length greater than 1 whenever one exists. With WITH p, the fast bidirectional breadth-first search returns one shortest path with no predicate; if its length is 1, the WHERE removes it and the query returns no rows, even though a valid path exists.

Неверный ответ может стоить баллов, так что отмечай только то, что можешь обосновать. Запомнить стоит слова слайда: "Always returns a result, if exists" против "Not guaranteed to return a result, even if it exists" (p. 1373).

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Exam of 11 Feb 2025, exercise 1 (Formula 1). ER: Person (Personal_ID, Name, Surname, Birth_Date, Gender) is the parent of Spectator (Favourite_Team), Team Owner and Team Member, ISA (T, E); Team Member is the parent of Engineer, Mechanic and Pilot (Championships_Won, Career), ISA (P, E). Race (Race_ID, Laps_Number, Max_Tickets, Date); Track (Location, Length [Km], Time_Record); Ticket (Ticket_ID, Price, Purchase_Date). Compete: Race (10:N) – Pilot (1:N), with Winner (Yes/No) and Fastest_Lap; On: Race (1:1) – Track (1:N); Attend: Ticket (1:1) – Race (1:N); Buy: Ticket (1:1) – Spectator (1:N).

"Consider the Pilot, Race, Track, Ticket, and Spectator entities from the ER model. Sketch a graph model/example describing the nodes, main attributes, and edges. Use the proper notation for labels, types, etc. (1 PT)"

Показать образец ответа 
// nodes
(:Person:TeamMember:Pilot {personal_id: String, name: String,
    surname: String, birth_date: Date, gender: String,
    championships_won: Integer, career: String})
(:Person:Spectator {personal_id: String, name: String,
    surname: String, birth_date: Date, gender: String,
    favourite_team: String})
(:Race {race_id: String, laps_number: Integer,
    max_tickets: Integer, date: Date})
(:Track {location: String, length_km: Float,
    time_record: Duration})
(:Ticket {ticket_id: String, price: Float, purchase_date: Date})
// edges
(:Pilot)-[:COMPETES {winner: Boolean,
    fastest_lap: Duration}]->(:Race)
(:Race)-[:ON]->(:Track)
(:Ticket)-[:ATTENDS]->(:Race)
(:Spectator)-[:BUYS]->(:Ticket)

A pilot is one node with the labels of both ancestors; there is no plain :Person node, because the top hierarchy is total. Compete's attributes are properties of the COMPETES relationship.

Что важно сделать правильно: двухуровневая ISA (три метки на пилоте, унаследованные атрибуты на узле), атрибуты связи на ребре, метки и типы записаны как метки и типы, одно направление на тип. Ticket остаётся узлом: у него свой ключ, свои атрибуты и две связи.

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Exam of 11 Feb 2025, exercise 1, continued. Same ER, and the model of the previous answer.

"Write a Cypher query to return the total number of races for each track in which at least 1'000 tickets were bought by female spectators, at least 500 of which have Ferrari as their favourite team. (2 PT)"

Показать образец ответа 
// per track: races with 1000+ female, 500+ Ferrari-fan tickets
MATCH (t:Track)<-[:ON]-(r:Race)<-[:ATTENDS]-(k:Ticket)
      <-[:BUYS]-(s:Spectator)
WHERE s.gender = "Female"
WITH t, r, count(DISTINCT k) AS female_tickets
WHERE female_tickets >= 1000
MATCH (r)<-[:ATTENDS]-(k2:Ticket)<-[:BUYS]-(s2:Spectator)
WHERE s2.gender = "Female" AND s2.favourite_team = "Ferrari"
WITH t, r, count(DISTINCT k2) AS ferrari_tickets
WHERE ferrari_tickets >= 500
RETURN t.location AS track, count(r) AS races

Assumption: gender is stored as "Female".

Два порога на два набора билетов одной и той же гонки: посчитать, отфильтровать, снова посчитать, снова отфильтровать — и только потом считать гонки на каждую трассу. Каждый WITH сохраняет t и r, иначе следующая клауза не смогла бы их использовать. Шаг, который решает ответ, — группировка по гонке, (t, r), до подсчёта по трассе.

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Exam of 13 Jun 2025, exercise 2 (concerts). ER: Person (Personal ID, Name, Surname, Birth Date) is the parent of Audience (Favourite Music Type), Artistic Director (Biography), Maestro and Musician, ISA (T, E). Concert (ID, Title, Start Time, Duration (minutes)); Location (Name, Address, Type, N Seats). Attend: Audience (1,N) – Concert (0,N); Organize: Artistic Director (1,N) – Concert (1,1); Located: Concert (1,1) – Location (1,N).

"Write a Cypher query to count the total number of concerts managed by each artistic director whose name is "Chiara", that lasted more than 100 minutes, and with more than 1000 attendances(2 PT)"

Показать образец ответа 
// each Chiara: concerts over 100 min with 1000+ attendees
MATCH (a:ArtisticDirector {name: "Chiara"})
      -[:ORGANIZES]->(c:Concert)<-[:ATTENDS]-(p:Audience)
WHERE c.duration > 100
WITH a, c, count(DISTINCT p) AS attendances
WHERE attendances > 1000
RETURN a.personal_id AS director, count(c) AS concerts

Model assumed: (:Person:ArtisticDirector)-[:ORGANIZES]->(:Concert)<-[:ATTENDS]-(:Person:Audience); "managed" is the ER's Organize.

Ловушка: у Concert нет атрибута посещаемости, так что "attendances" — это агрегат по концерту, первая стадия со своим WHERE, до подсчёта по дирижёру. Если считать концерты прямо по строкам посещений, получатся тысячи (2502 вместо 2 на тестовом графе). Группируй по узлу дирижёра, а не по имени: две Кьяры — это две строки.

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Exam of 6 Sep 2024, exercise 2 (restaurant). ER: Person (Personal_ID, Name, Surname, Birth Date, Phone Number) is the parent of Owner, Customer, Waiter (Hiring Date) and Chef. Table (Table_ID, Table Number, Max Seats); Dish (Dish_ID, Name, Price, Recipe, Description). Serve: Table (1:N) – Waiter (1:N); Order: Table (1:N) – Dish (1:N); Book: Table (1:N) – Customer (1:N), with Number of People and Booking Date. "N.B. … Tables are served by more Waiters overall, but only one at a time."

"Write a Cypher query to collect the total number of different dishes that were served at each table, considering only tables whose max seats is greater than 10 and served by at least one waiter whose name is "Federico" (1.5 PT)"

Показать образец ответа 
// tables over 10 seats served by a Federico: different dishes
MATCH (w:Waiter {name: "Federico"})-[:SERVES]->(t:Table)
WHERE t.max_seats > 10
WITH DISTINCT t
MATCH (t)-[:ORDERS]->(d:Dish)
RETURN t.table_id AS table, count(DISTINCT d) AS different_dishes

Model assumed: (:Person:Waiter)-[:SERVES]->(:Table)-[:ORDERS]->(:Dish); the dishes "served at" a table are the ER's Order.

Два условия на стол, одно из них — на соседа, затем подсчёт по другому соседу. WITH DISTINCT t не даёт двум Федерико, обслуживающим один стол, удвоить его строки, а count(DISTINCT d) отвечает на "different dishes". На тестовом графе без обоих DISTINCT у одного стола оказалось 4 блюда вместо 2.

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Explain how Cypher groups rows when a query uses an aggregate function, and why a condition such as "at least 5 different vehicles" cannot be written in the WHERE that directly follows MATCH. Compare with SQL.

Показать образец ответа

Cypher has no GROUP BY keyword. In a WITH or RETURN that contains aggregate functions, every expression that is not aggregated is a grouping key: the rows coming from MATCH are partitioned by the values of those keys, and each group becomes one row with its aggregates. So WITH p, count(DISTINCT v) AS n plays the role of SQL's GROUP BY p. The WHERE attached to MATCH filters single rows, before any group exists, so it cannot use count(); Neo4j rejects it as an invalid use of an aggregating function. The condition goes into a WHERE after the WITH that computes the aggregate, WITH p, count(DISTINCT v) AS n WHERE n >= 5, which corresponds to SQL's HAVING. Only the variables listed in that WITH are available afterwards.

Полный ответ говорит три вещи: неявные ключи, WHERE после WITH как HAVING и причину (строки раньше групп). Приложение (p. 1380) проводит сравнение с SQL именно в этих терминах, так что его словарь использовать безопасно. Новые версии Neo4j принимают и необязательный явный GROUP BY (см. Cypher Manual в ресурсах); курс и этот ответ используют неявное правило.

Урок 12 · WITH как GROUP BY: рецепт «ER → граф» и экзаменационный запрос · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Which statements about WITH in Cypher are true? Select all that apply.

  • Every expression of a WITH that is not inside an aggregate function becomes a grouping key. В приложении: "all non-aggregated variables in WITH become grouping keys" (p. 1380).
  • The grouping keys must be written before the aggregates inside the WITH. Позиция не важна: WITH count(*) AS c, person тоже группирует по person. Слайд (p. 1364) называет позицию "essential"; это привычка, а не правило.
  • A variable that is not listed in a WITH cannot be used in the clauses after it. Слайд (p. 1364): "All data that is not passed through to the WITH statement won't be available in the following part of the query."
  • A WHERE written right after a WITH can filter on an aggregate computed by that WITH. Это и есть HAVING в Cypher (p. 1380).
  • An aggregation always needs a WITH. RETURN тоже группирует: в RETURN u, count(re) (p. 1401) нет WITH.
Показать образец ответа

True: the first, the third and the fourth. The position of the keys is irrelevant, and RETURN can aggregate without any WITH.

За неправильные ответы на настоящем экзамене могут снимать баллы (об этом объявляют прямо перед ним), так что отмечай только то, что можешь обосновать. Второй вариант — это напечатанная формулировка слайда (p. 1364), превращённая в утверждение: знай, что слайд это говорит и что Neo4j этого не требует.

Модуль 4 · Документные базы данных
