Модуль 9 · Hadoop: MapReduce, HDFS и экосистема

26 вопросов · Лекции 11 и 12

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the scenario of the exam of 12 Sep 2025, which tracks farmers and their sales at local markets: Product (Product ID, Name, Quantity, Expiration Date) is sold at Market (Name, Location, Opening Time, Closing Time, Size) through the relationship Sell, with cardinality (1, N) on both sides. Each market stall logs the name of every product it sells, one word per sale, in a text file. Two stalls produced the files "milk cheese milk" and "cheese milk eggs", and each file is one input split.

Exemplify MapReduce with a word count over these files: give the output of each mapper, the input of the reducer after shuffle and sort (one reducer), and the output of the job. Then say how many pairs cross the network with and without a combiner.

Показать образец ответа

Mapper 1 (split "milk cheese milk") emits one pair per word: <milk, 1> <cheese, 1> <milk, 1>. Mapper 2 (split "cheese milk eggs"): <cheese, 1> <milk, 1> <eggs, 1>.

Shuffle and sort: all pairs with the same key go to the same reducer, which receives them sorted by key and grouped: <cheese, [1, 1]> <eggs, [1]> <milk, [1, 1, 1]>.

Reduce (sum of the values of each key): <cheese, 2> <eggs, 1> <milk, 3>. Check: the counts add up to 6, the number of words, and there is one output pair per distinct word.

Without a combiner, 6 pairs cross the network. With the reducer used as combiner, mapper 1 sends <milk, 2> <cheese, 1> and mapper 2 sends <cheese, 1> <milk, 1> <eggs, 1>: 5 pairs. The reducer then receives <milk, [2, 1]> and still outputs <milk, 3>.

Полный ответ показывает три этапа, каждый в своей форме: одна <word, 1> на каждое вхождение (в маппере никакого подсчёта), группы <word, [values]> после shuffle и сортировки, одна сумма на ключ. Записать <milk, 2> как выход map — классическая ошибка: это выход комбайнера.

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the scenario of the exam of 12 Sep 2025: People (Personal ID, Name, Surname, Age, Address, State) buy Products (Product ID, Name, Quantity, Expiration Date) through the relationship Buy, with attributes Buy Date and Amount. The purchases are exported as a large text file with one line per purchase: "PersonalID,ProductID,BuyDate,Amount".

Using the pseudo-code notation of the lecture, write map and reduce functions that compute, for each product, the total amount bought and the average amount per purchase. Can the reduce function also be used as the combiner? If not, write a combiner.

Показать образец ответа 
map(String key, String line):
    // key: offset of the line
    // line: "PersonalID,ProductID,BuyDate,Amount"
    (person, product, date, amount) = split(line, ",")
    EmitIntermediate(product, (ParseFloat(amount), 1));

combine(String product, Iterator pairs):
    // pairs: (partial sum, partial count)
    s = 0; c = 0
    for each (a, n) in pairs:
      s += a; c += n
    EmitIntermediate(product, (s, c));

reduce(String product, Iterator pairs):
    s = 0; c = 0
    for each (a, n) in pairs:
      s += a; c += n
    Emit(product, (s, s / c));

The map emits, for every purchase, the product as key and the pair (amount, 1) as value. The reduce adds up amounts and counts and emits the total and the average. The reduce cannot be the combiner: its output (total, average) has another meaning than its input (sum, count), and an average of partial averages is wrong when the parts differ in size — the average of 10 and 20 is 15, the average of 15 and 60 is 37.5, while the average of 10, 20 and 60 is 30. The combiner above emits partial (sum, count) pairs of the same type as the map output, so the framework may run it zero, one or several times without changing the result.

"Hadoop simple example" в стиле слайда (p. 810): ключ — атрибут группировки, значение несёт то, что нужно редьюсеру. Ловушка с комбайнером — средние не комбинируются — и есть суть вопроса; для одной только суммы комбайнером мог бы быть сам редьюсер.

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: when a worker fails, the map tasks it had already completed must be executed again, while the reduce tasks it had already completed are not re-executed.

  • True Выход завершённой map-задачи лежит на локальном диске упавшего воркера и теряется; выход завершённой reduce-задачи уже в распределённой файловой системе (p. 821).
  • False Асимметрия настоящая: "In-progress and Completed map tasks are re-scheduled", "Completed reduce tasks are not re-scheduled" (p. 821).
Показать образец ответа

True. A failure is detected by missing heartbeats. The completed map tasks of the failed worker stored their output on its local disk, which is no longer reachable, so they are re-scheduled together with the in-progress ones, and the workers executing reduce tasks are notified to read from the new location. Completed reduce tasks wrote their output to the distributed file system, so they are not re-executed.

Всё «почему» — в том, где лежит выход: на локальном диске для выхода map, в реплицированной файловой системе для выхода reduce. Вопрос «верно/неверно» провоцирует небрежное «неверно» у всех, кто рассуждает «завершена — значит, в безопасности».

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which component of a MapReduce job decides to which reducer an intermediate key-value pair is sent?

  • The combiner Комбайнер предварительно агрегирует выход одного маппера; редьюсер он не выбирает.
  • The input splitter Он режет вход на чанки для мапперов — ещё до того, как появилась хоть одна промежуточная пара.
  • The partitioner Он "Decides which pairs are sent to which reducer", по умолчанию через Key.hashCode() % numOfReducers, то есть hash(key) mod R (pp. 798, 825).
  • The output committer Он записывает выход reduce в файл — уже после того, как отработали редьюсеры.
Показать образец ответа

The partitioner (also called the shuffler): by default it computes hash(key) mod R, so all pairs with the same key reach the same reducer. It can be replaced, for example to obtain a total order of the output or a more uniform load.

Каждый неверный вариант — настоящий компонент, но не на своём месте в конвейере: знай порядок splitter, mapper, combiner, partitioner, reducer, committer.

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

What is a combiner in MapReduce? Why does it save network bandwidth, and which conditions must it satisfy to be used safely? Give one reduce function that can serve as a combiner and one that cannot.

Показать образец ответа

A combiner is an optional "intermediate reducer" that runs on the output of each mapper, on the map side, before the shuffle. When the map output contains many repeated keys — in a word count, thousands of <the, 1> — it merges the values of each key locally, so far fewer pairs are written, sorted and sent over the network to the reducers. Conditions: its input and output types must be the same as the map output types, because the reducer must accept both combined and uncombined pairs; it must have no side effects, because the framework may run it zero, one or several times; and the aggregation must give the same final result however the values are grouped, as for sums, counts of ones, maximum and minimum. The word-count reducer can be the combiner (conf.setCombinerClass(Reduce.class)); a reducer that computes an average cannot, unless the values travel as (sum, count) pairs and the division happens only in the reducer.

Три условия — те же типы, никаких побочных эффектов, агрегация, которой всё равно, как сгруппированы значения, — и два примера. Формулировка самого слайда "intermdiate number of times" означает неопределённое число раз: пиши "zero, one or more times".

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Explain the principle "move the code, not the data" in MapReduce. How does the Master use HDFS to schedule map tasks, and which data still crosses the network?

Показать образец ответа

Transferring a peta-scale input from where it is stored to the machines that run the map function would saturate the network, while the map code is tiny; so the framework sends the code to the machines that store the data. The Master asks the HDFS NameNode where the chunks (blocks) of the input are and assigns each map task to a worker that holds a copy of its split, which then reads from its local disk; if no such worker is free, it uses one close to the data, for example on the same network switch. The number of map tasks follows the block size, one split per block, to exploit this locality. The input then does not cross the network, but the intermediate data still does, in the shuffle: every reducer reads its partition from every map worker. Combiners and compression of the map output reduce this traffic.

Полный ответ даёт механизм (Master спрашивает у NameNode расположение блоков), запасной вариант (тот же свитч) и честное ограничение (shuffle); если остановиться на лозунге, все три пропадут.

Урок 30 · MapReduce: фабрика соков · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements about MapReduce agree with the lecture? Select all that apply.

  • The number of map tasks M is specified by the user, while the number of reduce tasks R is determined by the system. Наоборот: система определяет M по сплитам, пользователь задаёт R (p. 808).
  • Speculative execution runs extra copies of slow tasks near the end of a job, and the output of the copy that finishes first is used. pp. 818 и 824: резервные задачи, когда задание близко к завершению, побеждает первая закончившая.
  • By default, Hadoop schedules jobs with the Fair scheduler, developed by Facebook. По умолчанию — FIFO (p. 829); Fair и Capacity — альтернативы.
  • Each reduce task produces one output file. Так сказано на слайде (p. 823), и поэтому R задаёт ещё и число выходных файлов.
  • A combiner may change the types of the mapper output, since the reducer converts them back. "Cannot change the type of its input": его входные и выходные типы должны совпадать (p. 799).
Показать образец ответа

The second and the fourth statements.

Вопрос «выбери все верные», где лишняя галочка может стоить баллов. Неверные варианты меняют местами две роли (M и R), называют не тот дефолт (FIFO, а не Fair) и нарушают правило типов комбайнера.

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

True or false: in HDFS, a client that writes a file sends each block to the NameNode, which then stores it on three DataNodes.

  • True NameNode никогда не получает данные файла: он регистрирует файл (create, complete) и выдаёт клиенту список DataNode для каждого блока (pp. 862, 868).
  • False Клиент пишет каждый блок один раз, на первый DataNode конвейера, который пересылает его на второй, а второй — на третий; подтверждения идут обратно (p. 862).
Показать образец ответа

False. The NameNode handles only metadata. The client calls create() and the NameNode adds the file to the namespace; for each block the client receives a list of DataNodes chosen by the placement policy. The client streams the block, in packets, to the first DataNode only; the first DataNode forwards the data to the second and the second to the third (data pipelining), and acknowledgements travel back along the pipeline. When all replicas are written the client moves on to the next block; at the end it closes the stream and the NameNode is told that the file is complete. Keeping the NameNode out of the data path is what gives HDFS its high aggregate bandwidth.

Ловушка — NameNode рядом со словом "stores". Полный ответ добавляет конвейер: одна загрузка на блок, три копии, подтверждения идут обратно.

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements agree with the lecture's comparison between an RDBMS and Hadoop? Select all that apply.

  • Hadoop targets petabytes of data, an RDBMS gigabytes. Строка "Data size" со слайда (p. 840).
  • Hadoop supports both interactive and batch access. "Interactive & Batch" — это столбец RDBMS; у Hadoop — "Batch".
  • Hadoop follows a write-once, read-many-times model for updates. Строка "Updates" и модель "write-once-read-many" со слайдов (pp. 837 и 851).
  • "Integrity: Low" means that HDFS often corrupts the data it stores. Это значит: ни схемы, ни ограничений, ни транзакций; HDFS проверяет каждый блок контрольной суммой CRC32 (p. 860).
  • Hadoop scales linearly, an RDBMS non-linearly. Строка "Scaling": каждая машина хранит и обрабатывает свою долю.
Показать образец ответа

The first, third and fifth statements.

Вопрос «выбери все верные», где лишняя галочка может стоить баллов. Два неверных варианта — классические ошибки чтения: перепутанный столбец доступа и "low integrity", прочитанное как «теряет данные».

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

A hospital heating management system stores its sensor readings in HDFS as one file of 200 MB. With the defaults printed on the slides — block size 64 MB, replication factor 3 — how many block replicas does the cluster store for this file?

Показать образец ответа
\[ n = \left\lceil \frac{200}{64} \right\rceil = 4 \] \[ 4 \times 3 = 12 \]

Four blocks (64 + 64 + 64 + 8 MB), three replicas each: 12 block replicas, which occupy 3 × 200 MB = 600 MB of disk.

Две ловушки: округлить 3.1 вниз до 3 блоков и посчитать диск как 4 × 64 × 3 = 768 MB — последний блок содержит только свои 8 MB.

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Describe the architecture of Hadoop and its main components, distinguishing the core of Hadoop from its ecosystem.

Показать образец ответа

Hadoop is a software platform for storing and processing very large data sets in batch on clusters of commodity machines; it is an open-source implementation of Google's GFS and MapReduce. Its core has four parts: Hadoop Common (shared libraries), HDFS (storage), YARN (resource management, since Hadoop 2) and MapReduce (batch processing). HDFS has a master/worker architecture: one NameNode keeps the namespace in memory — the blocks of each file and the DataNodes of each block — while the DataNodes store the blocks (64 MB each on the slides, replicated three times), send heartbeats and block reports, and serve data directly to clients. YARN has one Resource Manager for the cluster's resources, a NodeManager on every machine that launches and monitors containers, and one Application Master per application that negotiates containers and schedules and monitors its tasks; in Hadoop 1 both roles belonged to the JobTracker, with a TaskTracker per node. Every worker runs a DataNode and a NodeManager, so computation can be moved to the data. Around the core, the ecosystem adds separate projects: Hive (SQL queries), Pig (scripting), HBase (a column store for random access), ZooKeeper (coordination), Sqoop (data exchange with relational databases), Flume (log collection), Oozie (workflows), Mahout (machine learning), Hue (web interface) and Ambari (cluster management), packaged by distributions such as Cloudera's CDH or the Hortonworks Data Platform.

Вопрос использует слова самих правил экзамена. Сначала дай ядро с ролью каждого мастера и воркера, потом экосистему по ролям; перечисление названий без ролей — слабый ответ.

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

What metadata does the HDFS NameNode keep, and where? What are the transaction log and the Secondary NameNode for? Why is the NameNode a single point of failure in HDFS 1, and how does Hadoop 2 address it?

Показать образец ответа

The NameNode manages the file-system namespace: it maps each file name to its list of blocks and each block to the DataNodes holding its replicas, and keeps file attributes such as creation time and replication factor. The entire metadata is in main memory, with no demand paging, so every lookup is fast and the number of files is bounded by RAM. Every change to the namespace, such as a file creation or deletion, is recorded in a transaction log, while the FsImage is the saved snapshot of the namespace. The Secondary NameNode periodically copies the FsImage and the transaction log, merges them into a new FsImage and uploads it to the NameNode, whose log is then purged: it is a checkpointing helper that keeps the log short and the restart fast, not a standby. In HDFS 1 there is a single NameNode, so when it fails the whole file system is unavailable; storing the transaction log in several directories, one on a remote NFS/CIFS file system, only prevents losing the namespace. Hadoop 2 adds an Active and a Standby NameNode: the active one writes every edit to a shared edit log, the standby replays it, and the DataNodes report to both, so the standby can take over.

Проверяются четыре вещи: две карты в RAM, журнал и FsImage, Secondary как checkpointer — самый частый неверный ответ называет его резервным NameNode — и standby в Hadoop 2.

Урок 31 · Hadoop и HDFS: блоки, реплики, один очень важный сервер, YARN · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

What were the limitations of Hadoop 1.0, and how does YARN overcome them?

Показать образец ответа

In Hadoop 1.0 a single JobTracker managed the resources of the whole cluster and also scheduled and monitored every task of every job, while a TaskTracker on each node ran the tasks in map and reduce slots. Scalability was limited to about 4000 nodes and 40000 concurrent tasks, because of coarse synchronization in the JobTracker; the JobTracker was a single point of failure whose failure killed all queued and running jobs, which users had to resubmit; restarting it was tricky because of its complex state; and only MapReduce could run. YARN splits the two functions of the JobTracker: a global Resource Manager does only cluster resource management, and a per-application Application Master, itself running in a container, negotiates resource containers from the scheduler and schedules and monitors its own job; the TaskTracker is replaced by a per-node NodeManager that launches containers, monitors their CPU, memory, disk and network use and reports to the Resource Manager. As a result clusters reach 6,000–10,000 machines and more than 100,000 concurrent tasks; the Resource Manager saves its state in ZooKeeper and restarts the Application Masters automatically, and MapReduce applications resume from state saved in HDFS; protocols are wire-compatible, allowing rolling upgrades; and other paradigms — Tez, Spark, Giraph, Storm, HBase — run on the same cluster through their own Application Masters, while existing MapReduce jobs keep working.

Сопоставь каждое ограничение со слайда (p. 842) с ответом на него: масштабируемость — с разделением и числами со слайда (p. 884); единую точку отказа и сложный перезапуск — с маленьким состоянием Resource Manager, сохранённым в ZooKeeper, перезапускаемыми Application Master-ами и состоянием MapReduce в HDFS; «только MapReduce» — с Application Master-ами для конкретных парадигм. Wire compatibility и rolling upgrades — бонусный балл.

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 1 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Describe the data model of HBase, using the zoo example of the slides. How is a cell identified, how is a table stored, and what does "nulls are free" mean?

Показать образец ответа

HBase is modelled on Google's Bigtable: a key-valued row/column store on HDFS for billions of rows and millions of columns. A table is a sparse map in which each cell is addressed by its row key, its column — written family:qualifier — and a timestamp, and holds an untyped byte array. Column families are declared with the table (in the zoo example, animal: and repairs:), while qualifiers such as animal:type and animal:size can be added by any row. Every write creates a new version: enclosure1 has animal:type = lion at t1 and zebra at t2, and a read returns the newest version, zebra. Each column family is stored separately, as a list of (row, timestamp, column) → value entries: three in animal: and one in repairs: for the example. A cell that was never written has no entry at all, so it takes no space — that is "nulls are free"; space grows with the cells written, not with rows times columns. Access is only by key: get a cell or a row, or scan a range of row keys; there is no SQL and no join.

Полный ответ покрывает четыре вещи: адрес ячейки (row key, family:qualifier, timestamp) с нетипизированным значением; версии, из которых возвращается самая новая; хранение по семействам как записи (row, timestamp, column) → value; отсутствующую запись как смысл "nulls are free". Используй зоопарк: это собственный пример слайдов. Если упоминаешь "column-oriented", добавь, что речь о семействах столбцов, а не о колоночном хранилище.

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 2 из 6 · открыть в уроке

The Pig Latin script below finds the top 5 most visited pages by users aged 18–25. Into how many MapReduce jobs is it compiled, which statements belong to each job, and why that number?

Users = load 'users' as (name, age);
Filtered = filter Users by age >= 18 and age <= 25;
Pages = load 'pages' as (user, url);
Joined = join Filtered by name, Pages by user;
Grouped = group Joined by url;
Summed = foreach Grouped generate group, COUNT(Joined) as clicks;
Sorted = order Summed by clicks desc;
Top5 = limit Sorted 5;
store Top5 into 'top5sites';
Показать образец ответа

Three MapReduce jobs. A MapReduce job has at most one shuffle, so Pig starts a new job for every operator that needs tuples with equal keys brought together across the cluster: the join on name, the group on url and the order by clicks. Job 1 loads users and pages and filters the users by age in the map phase, shuffles both inputs on the name and joins them in the reduce phase. Job 2 reads the joined tuples, shuffles them on url, and counts each group in the reduce phase (COUNT can also run as a combiner). Job 3 shuffles the (url, clicks) pairs on clicks, so they arrive sorted; the reduce side keeps the first five and stores them. LOAD, FILTER, FOREACH … GENERATE and LIMIT need no shuffle of their own and ride along in the map or reduce phase of these jobs; the outputs of jobs 1 and 2 are written to HDFS and read by the next job.

Одно число не отвечает на «почему»: назови правило — одно задание на shuffle — и распредели операторы по заданиям, как на слайде (p. 904). Соблазнительные неверные ответы — девять (по одному на строку) и одно («это же один скрипт»). Если знаешь, что настоящий Pig добавляет перед ORDER маленькое задание-выборку, скажи об этом после трёх заданий со слайда (p. 904), но никогда не вместо них.

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 3 из 6 · открыть в уроке

A Hive table page_views is PARTITIONED BY (dt STRING, country STRING) and holds 365 days of data for 4 countries, one partition per (dt, country) pair. How many partition directories does Hive read to answer the query below?

SELECT * FROM page_views
WHERE dt = '2008-03-31' AND referrer_url LIKE '%xyz.com';
  • 1 Для этого нужно было бы условие ещё и на country. С одним dt читаются все страны этого дня.
  • 4 dt = '2008-03-31' выбирает один день; country не ограничен, поэтому читаются 4 каталога этого дня, а LIKE фильтрует строки внутри них.
  • 124 31 день × 4 страны: весь март — это то, что выбирает напечатанный запрос со слайда (p. 911), а не этот.
  • 1460 Вся таблица: так бывает, только если условия на dt нет. LIKE на referrer_url ничего не отсекает.
Показать образец ответа

4 directories out of 365 × 4 = 1460. Partition pruning matches the condition on the partition column dt against the partitions listed in the Metastore before the job starts, so only the directories dt=2008-03-31/country=… become input. The condition on referrer_url concerns a column inside the files: it is evaluated row by row in the mappers and does not reduce the data read.

Две идеи в одном вопросе: партиция — это каталог, выбранный по значению до запуска задания, а условие на обычный столбец ничего не отсекает.

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 4 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Impala and Hive can query the same tables. Explain why Impala answers interactive queries much faster than Hive, what it gives up in exchange, and when Hive remains the better choice.

Показать образец ответа

Hive compiles a HiveQL query into MapReduce jobs: every job pays a start-up cost, and the output of every job is written to HDFS before the next one reads it. Impala is a massively parallel processing (MPP) engine integrated into the Hadoop stack on the same level as MapReduce, not above it: long-running daemons on the DataNodes plan the query, split it into fragments that run in parallel on every node, read the HDFS blocks locally and stream intermediate rows to each other in memory, and its scanners push predicates down into the read. It shares Hive's Metastore, so it uses the same tables and files without moving any data — the engine is brought to the data. The slides claim it is always at least 10 times faster than Hive. In exchange it has no fault tolerance within a query (the slide says "inter-query"): if a node fails, the whole query is aborted and must be restarted, whereas MapReduce re-runs only the failed tasks because their inputs and outputs are on disk. In the slides' version Impala also could not join several big tables, had no Java UDFs and was not elastic in the cloud. Hive therefore remains the choice for long batch jobs over very large tables, where a restart would be expensive, and Impala for short interactive queries.

Суть ответа — компромисс, а не скорость: механизм (MPP-демоны, без MapReduce, стриминг в памяти, локальное чтение, predicate pushdown), цена (сбой узла убивает запрос — внутри запроса, что бы ни говорило напечатанное "inter-query") и следствие (длинные задания — на Hive, интерактивные — на Impala). Остальные пункты слайда (p. 921) устарели: используя их, говори «на слайдах».

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 5 из 6 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements are true? Select all that apply.

  • Pig and Hive run their queries as MapReduce jobs, whereas Impala runs SQL without MapReduce. Верно: Pig и Hive стоят поверх MapReduce; Impala стоит на том же уровне и обрабатывает данные "without using MapReduce" (pp. 915–916).
  • HBase stores a NULL marker for every unwritten cell of a row. Неверно: у незаписанной ячейки нет записи вовсе — "nulls are free" (pp. 893, 895).
  • Hive keeps the schemas of its tables in the Metastore and their data as files in HDFS directories. Верно: "Maintains list of table schemas" (p. 906); каталог хранилища, подкаталоги и плоские файлы (p. 909).
  • Tables created in Hive must be redefined in Impala before Impala can query them. Неверно: "Impala shares metastore with Hive, which enables very simple adoption" (p. 919).
  • A Hive bucket spreads the rows of a partition over a fixed number of files by hashing a column. Верно: "Hash partitions within ranges (useful for sampling, join optimization)" (p. 908).
  • "Can call Hadoop Streaming scripts from HiveQL" means that Hive processes real-time data streams. Неверно: Hadoop Streaming запускает мапперы и редьюсеры, написанные на любом языке, через стандартный ввод и вывод; потоковая обработка — это Storm.
Показать образец ответа

True: the first, the third and the fifth. HBase does not store anything for an unwritten cell; Impala shares the Hive Metastore, so Hive's tables are visible to it at once; Hadoop Streaming is a way of writing MapReduce code in any language, not stream processing.

При возможном штрафе отмечай только то, что подтверждает слайд. В каждом неверном утверждении подменено слово: NULL marker вместо отсутствия записи, redefine вместо share, streaming scripts вместо потоковой обработки.

Урок 32 · Экосистема Hadoop: HBase, Pig, Hive, Impala · вопрос 6 из 6 · открыть в уроке

An exercise of ours, on the ER diagram of the Exam of 12 Sep 2025 (tracking farmers and their sales at local markets): People (Personal ID, Name, Surname, Age, Address, State) buy Products (Product ID, Name, Quantity, Expiration Date) through the relationship Buy (Buy Date, Amount); Products are sold at Markets (Name, Location, Opening Time, Closing Time, Size) through the relationship Sell.

Suppose you record a large, continuously increasing number of purchases of products at the markets as files in HDFS, and analyse them with Hive, mostly one day at a time. (a) Write the Hive table for the purchases, with the attributes of the ER model you need, partitioned so that a query on one day reads only that day's data. (b) Write a HiveQL query that returns, for each market location, the number of distinct people who bought a product on 20/03/2025 and the total amount. (c) How many partition directories does your query read, and why?

Показать образец ответа 
CREATE TABLE purchases(product_id STRING, product_name STRING,
                       market_name STRING, market_location STRING,
                       personal_id STRING, amount DOUBLE)
COMMENT 'One row per purchase of a product at a market'
PARTITIONED BY(buy_date STRING)
STORED AS SEQUENCEFILE;
SELECT market_location, COUNT(DISTINCT personal_id), SUM(amount)
FROM purchases
WHERE buy_date = '2025-03-20'
GROUP BY market_location;

(a) The partition column buy_date is declared in PARTITIONED BY and not in the column list: each day becomes a directory such as /user/hive/warehouse/purchases/buy_date=2025-03-20/. Dates are stored as yyyy-mm-dd strings, which sort in date order. (b) WHERE comes before GROUP BY; COUNT(DISTINCT personal_id) counts people, not purchases. (c) One directory: buy_date is a partition column, so the compiler matches the WHERE against the partitions listed in the Metastore before the job starts and reads only buy_date=2025-03-20; the GROUP BY then needs one shuffle, on market_location.

Упражнение наше: Hive не входит в темы практической части (Neo4j, MongoDB, Elasticsearch, Redis, SparkQL), но первое упражнение самого варианта просит колоночный дизайн для тех же продаж. Оно проверяет то, о чём теоретическая часть может спросить про Hive: столбцы партиционирования, объявленные отдельно и превращённые в каталоги, WHERE перед GROUP BY и COUNT(DISTINCT …), считающий людей. Используются только атрибуты ER-модели, как того требует упражнение на MongoDB из того же варианта ("Do not add variables besides the ones included in the ER model").

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 1 из 7 · открыть в уроке

A Storm topology counts the occurrences of each word in a stream of sentences. A bolt splits the sentences and emits one tuple per word to a counting bolt with several tasks, each keeping a counter per word. Which stream grouping lets the counting tasks share the work while the count of each word stays exact in a single task?

  • Shuffle grouping Работу она делит, но кортежи одного слова попадают в случайные задачи: каждая задача хранит частичный счёт, и итога не хранит ни одна.
  • All grouping Каждая задача получает каждый кортеж и считает всё: счётчики точные, но вычисляются в каждой задаче, так что работа не делится.
  • Fields grouping on the field "word" Задача выбирается по хешу слова ("consistent hashing on a subset of tuple fields", p. 933): одно и то же слово всегда попадает в одну и ту же задачу, а разные слова расходятся по задачам.
  • Global grouping Все кортежи идут в задачу с наименьшим id: счётчики точные, но всю работу делает одна задача.
Показать образец ответа

Fields grouping on "word". The tuple is routed by a hash of the chosen field, so all tuples with the same word reach the same task, which therefore holds the exact count for that word, while different words are spread over the tasks. Shuffle grouping would split each word's count over several tasks; all grouping would make every task count everything; global grouping would send everything to one task.

Суть вопроса — связь группировки и состояния: bolt, который хранит состояние по ключу, должен получать кортежи этого ключа в одном месте. Каждый неверный вариант прав в чём-то другом — shuffle для bolts без состояния, all для рассылки всем, global для одного итога, — поэтому они и соблазнительны.

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 2 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Describe the main concepts of Apache Storm — stream, spout, bolt, topology — and the components of a Storm cluster. In what does a Storm topology differ from a MapReduce job?

Показать образец ответа

Storm is a distributed system for realtime computation, developed at BackType, which Twitter acquired. A stream is an unbounded sequence of tuples, a tuple being a named list of values. A spout is a source of streams: it reads data from an external source and emits tuples. A bolt processes one or more input streams and produces new streams; it can implement filters, aggregations, joins or any function. A topology is a network of spouts and bolts — a directed graph — and every component runs as many tasks across the cluster; a stream grouping (shuffle, fields, all, global) decides which task receives each tuple. The cluster has a master node, Nimbus, similar to Hadoop's JobTracker, which distributes the code, assigns tasks to machines and monitors failures; a ZooKeeper cluster used for coordination, which keeps the cluster state; and Supervisors on the worker machines, which run the worker processes. A MapReduce job reads a bounded input and finishes; a topology processes an unbounded stream with low latency and runs until it is killed.

Полный ответ — это четыре определения, три компонента и одно отличие «заканчивается / никогда не заканчивается». Если есть место, добавь, зачем ZooKeeper стоит между Nimbus и Supervisors: собственного состояния они не хранят, так что любой можно перезапустить, а топологии продолжают работать, пока Nimbus лежит (туториал Storm).

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 3 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Explain how Apache Flume moves events from a source to a sink within an agent, and from one agent to the next, without losing them. What does this mechanism not guarantee?

Показать образец ответа

A Flume agent hosts a source, a channel and a sink. The source accepts incoming data, turns it into events (headers with metadata, used for routing, and a body with the content) and writes them into the channel inside a transaction, which commits only when the channel has accepted the whole batch. The sink polls the channel and removes a batch inside its own transaction, and commits only after a successful transfer — when the destination, such as HDFS, has written it, or when the source of the next agent has committed it into its own channel. On any failure the transaction rolls back and the events stay in the channel to be retried. An event therefore leaves a channel only when the next hop holds it, and no event is lost in transit between agents. Not guaranteed: durability with a memory channel, which is not persistent — its events are lost if the agent process dies, so only a file (disk) channel survives a restart; exactly-once delivery, since a batch retried after a failed commit can arrive twice; and strict ordering, since Flume gives only a weak ordering guarantee: events arrive in the order received in the absence of failures.

Слайды печатают "This ensures no data loss" (p. 949): повтори это, а потом добавь условия — красную ручку этого урока. Вопрос просит обе половины: сначала механизм, потом три ограничения.

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 4 из 7 · открыть в уроке

True or false: Apache Flume guarantees that events always reach the destination in the order in which the source received them.

  • True Только "in the absence of failures": итоговый слайд о Flume называет это "weak ordering guarantee". Если агент умирает и его веб-серверы переключаются на второй агент (p. 955), события из file channel первого агента приходят после более новых.
  • False Слайд (p. 986): "Flume provides weak ordering guarantee, i.e., in the absence of failures the data will arrive in the order it was received". Именно «Always» делает утверждение ложным.
Показать образец ответа

False. Flume provides only a weak ordering guarantee: in the absence of failures, events arrive in the order in which they were received. After a failure — for example an agent that dies and whose producers fail over to another agent — events can arrive out of order.

Ищи абсолютное слово: гарантия со слайда действует только без сбоев. "flow ordering*" со слайда (p. 951) несёт то же ограничение в своей звёздочке.

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 5 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Consider the farmers' market system of the Exam of 12 Sep 2025: farmers sell their products at local markets, and every sale is sent as an event to a tier of Flume agents that writes into HDFS. In the worst case the tier ingests 1,500 events per second, and HDFS may be unavailable for up to 40 minutes. Following the rule of thumb of the slides, what channel capacity does the tier need, in millions of events?

Показать образец ответа

Channel capacity = worst-case ingest rate × worst-case downstream outage = 1,500 events/s × 2,400 s = 3,600,000 events, i.e. 3.6 million events, spread over the agents of the tier. At this size a file channel is needed, on several disks, with more disk space than the raw data.

Правило со слайда (p. 963) в формулировке итогового слайда (p. 964); сценарий взят из варианта, вопрос наш. Обычная ошибка — забыть перевести минуты: 1 500 × 40 = 60 000 событий покрывают всего 40 секунд простоя.

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 6 из 7 · открыть в уроке

Which of the following statements about Apache Flume and Apache Sqoop are true? Select all that apply.

  • In a Flume agent, a source may write to several channels, while a sink reads from exactly one channel. Верно: "Source – Channel: many-to-many", "Channel– Sink: one-to-many (sink reads only from one channel)" (p. 966).
  • The Flume channel pushes events to the sink as soon as they arrive. Неверно: "Source pushes events", "Sink polls for events" (p. 946); channel пассивен.
  • With a memory channel, the events waiting in the channel survive a crash of the agent process. Неверно: memory channel — "Not persistent" (p. 968); события через перезапуск сохраняет только file (disk) channel.
  • The multiplexing channel selector routes each event to channels according to the value of one of its headers. Верно: "routes events to channels based on their headers" (p. 981), например заголовок show = 1 — в ch1 (p. 982).
  • Sqoop is pull-based: it reads the relational database in bulk when the import runs. Верно: "PULL-based (no streams)", "Bulk imports" (p. 988).
  • Sqoop streams every new row of a table into HDFS as soon as the row is inserted. Неверно: "no streams" (p. 988); инкрементальное задание забирает новые строки, только когда его запускают.
Показать образец ответа

True: the first, the fourth and the fifth. The sink polls the channel; a memory channel is not persistent; Sqoop imports in bulk when it is run, not as a stream.

В каждом неверном утверждении подменено одно слово слайда: push вместо poll, survives вместо "Not persistent", streams вместо "no streams". При возможном штрафе отмечай только то, что подтверждает фраза со слайда.

Урок 33 · Потоки в Hadoop: Storm, Flume и Sqoop · вопрос 7 из 7 · открыть в уроке

An exercise in writing a configuration and a command. Consider the ER model of the hospital heating management system of the Exam of 30 Jan 2026: a Hospital (Hospital_ID, Name) has Buildings (Building_ID, Name, Year), Departments (Department_ID, Specialty, N_Beds, Head_Physician_ID) and Heating Systems (Heater_Sys_ID, Heating_Power), which heat Floors. Every heating system posts a reading each minute as a JSON event over HTTP, with a header building that holds its Building_ID. The Department table, whose primary key is the integer Department_ID, lives in the hospital's MySQL database hospitaldb.

(a) Write the properties file of a Flume agent heatagent that receives the readings on port 8989 and writes them as plain text files into HDFS under /hospital/readings, one folder per building, without losing the readings held by the agent if its process restarts. (b) Write the Sqoop command that copies the Department table into the HDFS directory /hospital/departments with two mappers.

Показать образец ответа 
heatagent.sources = http-source
heatagent.sinks = hdfs-sink
heatagent.channels = ch

heatagent.sources.http-source.channels = ch
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.channel = ch

heatagent.sources.http-source.type = org.apache.flume.source.http.HTTPSource
heatagent.sources.http-source.bind = 0.0.0.0
heatagent.sources.http-source.port = 8989

heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.type = hdfs
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.hdfs.path = /hospital/readings/%{building}
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.hdfs.filePrefix = readings
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.hdfs.fileSuffix = .log
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.hdfs.inUsePrefix = _
heatagent.sinks.hdfs-sink.hdfs.fileType = DataStream

heatagent.channels.ch.type = file
sqoop import \
--connect jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/hospitaldb \
--username=admin \
--password=password \
--table=Department \
--target-dir /hospital/departments \
-m 2

(a) The agent is started with flume-ng agent --conf-file heatagent.properties --name heatagent. %{building} puts each reading in the folder of its header value; DataStream writes plain text; the file channel keeps the waiting readings on disk across a restart, which a memory channel would not. (b) The two mappers split the rows by ranges of the primary key Department_ID, so no --split-by is needed; each writes one file into the new directory.

Упражнение наше, в обстановке прошлого варианта; до сих пор ни один вариант не просил код Flume или Sqoop. Оно проверяет три идеи: ключи проводки (channels у source, channel у sink), bucketing по заголовку в пути HDFS и file channel как ответ на «без потерь». bind = 0.0.0.0 принимает запросы с других машин; localhost со слайда — только локальные.
